];

/* =========================================================
   Big view tools (move / paint / zoom)
   ========================================================= */
function brushColor() {
  if (ui.brush.color) return ui.brush.color;
  return ui.brush.tool === "gold" ? "#D4AF37" : design.style === "clear" ? "#29B7A3" : design.colors[0];
}
function renderStudioTools() {
  const box = els.studioTools;
  if (!ui.full) { box.innerHTML = ""; return; }
  const B = (k) => t("big." + k);
  const seg = (action, val, cur, label) => `<button type="button" class="chip ${cur === val ? "on" : ""}" aria-pressed="${cur === val}" data-tool="${action}" data-value="${val}">${label}</button>`;
  const cols = [...new Set([...design.colors, "#FFFFFF", "#D4AF37", ...CONFIG.colors.map((c) => c.hex)].map((c) => c.toUpperCase()))];
  const bc = brushColor().toUpperCase();
  const gl = ui.step === GLITTER_STEP && design.style !== "ocean";
  const zoom = `<label class="zoom"><span>${B("zoom")}</span><input type="range" min="1" max="4" step="0.25" value="${ui.zoom}" data-tool="zoom" aria-label="${B("zoom")}"></label>`;
  if (!gl) { box.innerHTML = `<div class="tool-row">${zoom}</div>`; return; }
  const cur = ui.pour.id;
  box.innerHTML = `
    <div class="ft-title">${U("sGlH")}</div>
    <div class="ft-grid">${CONFIG.glitters.map((g) => `<button type="button" class="ft-g ${cur === g.id ? "on" : ""}" data-glitter="${g.id}" title="${esc(tr(g, "name"))}" aria-label="${esc(tr(g, "name"))}" aria-pressed="${cur === g.id}"><img src="images/glitter/${g.id}.png" alt=""></button>`).join("")}</div>
    <div class="ft-sizes">${["s", "m", "l"].map((v) => `<button type="button" class="chip brush ${ui.pour.size === v ? "on" : ""}" data-pour="size" data-value="${v}" title="${U("glSz")[v]}"><span class="brush-dot" style="width:${{ s: 6, m: 14, l: 26 }[v]}px;height:${{ s: 6, m: 14, l: 26 }[v]}px"></span></button>`).join("")}</div>
    <div class="ft-actions">
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-line small" data-action="glfill" ${cur ? "" : "disabled"}>${U("glFill")}</button>
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-line small" data-action="glundo" ${ui.pourHistory.length ? "" : "disabled"}>${U("glUndo")}</button>
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-line small" data-action="glclear" ${design.crush.length ? "" : "disabled"}>${U("glClear")}</button>
    </div>
    ${zoom}`;
}

/* =========================================================
   Rendering and events
   ========================================================= */
const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
const els = {
  stage: $("stage"), preview: $("preview"), panel: $("panel"), steps: $("steps"), hint: $("stage-hint"),
  total: $("total"), back: $("back"), next: $("next"), toast: $("toast"),
  toastText: $("toast-text"), toastAction: $("toast-action"), studioTools: $("studio-tools"),
};

const GLITTER_STEP = 6;
const DETAILS_STEP = 5;
const inking = () => ui.step === DETAILS_STEP && design.style === "ink" && design.picked.length > 0 && !(false);
const pouring = () => !!ui.pour.id && ui.step === GLITTER_STEP && !(false);
function hasDraggables() {
  return activeAddins(design).some((a) => a.drag);
}
function renderHint() {
  let h = "";
  if (ui.side === "back") h = ui.full ? t("big.backHint") : "";
  else if (pouring()) h = U("glHint", { name: glitterName(ui.pour.id) });
  else if (inking()) h = U("inkHint");
  else if (ui.full) h = hasDraggables() ? t("big.moveHint") : "";
  else if (hasDraggables()) h = U("dragHint");
  els.hint.textContent = h;
}
let showTimer = 0;
function showcaseHtml() {
  const seen = new Set(), list = [];
  CONFIG.presets.forEach((p) => { if (!seen.has(p.photo)) { seen.add(p.photo); list.push([p.photo, p.name]); } });
  Object.values(CONFIG.references || {}).forEach((arr) => arr.forEach((r) => { if (r.img && !seen.has(r.img)) { seen.add(r.img); list.push([r.img, r.cap]); } }));
  list.push(list[0]);
  return `<div class="showcase"><div class="show-track">${list.map(([im, c]) => `<figure><img src="${im}" alt="" decoding="async"><figcaption>${esc(c)}</figcaption></figure>`).join("")}</div><p class="show-note">${U("showNote")}</p></div>`;
}
function startShowcase() {
  clearInterval(showTimer);
  const tr = els.preview.querySelector(".show-track"); if (!tr) return;
  const n = tr.children.length; let at = 0;
  tr.addEventListener("transitionend", () => { if (at >= n - 1) { tr.style.transition = "none"; at = 0; tr.style.transform = "translateX(0)"; void tr.offsetWidth; tr.style.transition = ""; } });
  showTimer = setInterval(() => { if (document.hidden) return; at += 1; tr.style.transform = `translateX(-${at * 100}%)`; }, 4200);
}
function startCatSlides() {
  clearInterval(startCatSlides.t);
  const sl = [...els.panel.querySelectorAll(".cat-slides .slides")];
  if (!sl.length) return;
  const st = sl.map((el, i) => ({ el, n: el.children.length, at: 0, wait: i }));
  st.forEach((s) => s.el.addEventListener("transitionend", () => { if (s.at >= s.n - 1) { s.el.style.transition = "none"; s.at = 0; s.el.style.transform = "translateX(0)"; void s.el.offsetWidth; s.el.style.transition = ""; } }));
  startCatSlides.t = setInterval(() => { if (document.hidden) return; st.forEach((s) => { if (s.n < 2) return; if (s.wait-- > 0) return; s.at += 1; s.el.style.transform = `translateX(-${s.at * 100}%)`; }); }, 3600);
}
const showingShowcase = () => (ui.step === 0 || (ui.step === 1 && !ui.chosen)) && !ui.full;
function renderPreview() {
  syncBoard(design);
  document.body.classList.toggle("noprev", showingShowcase());
  if (showingShowcase()) { els.preview.innerHTML = ""; els.stage.classList.add("showing"); return; }
  els.stage.classList.remove("showing");
  els.preview.innerHTML = renderPiece(design, ui.side, { interactive: true, zoom: ui.zoom, pan: ui.pan });
  els.preview.classList.toggle("painting", false && ui.side === "front");
  renderHint();
  if (typeof markSelected === "function") markSelected();
}
function renderTotal() {
  const box = els.total.parentNode;
  box.hidden = !SHOW_PRICES;
  if (SHOW_PRICES) els.total.textContent = money(priceOf(design).total);
}
function renderSteps() {
  const names = t("steps");
  const ord = stepOrder() || names.map((_, k) => k);
  els.steps.innerHTML = ord.map((i) => [names[i], i]).map(([name, i]) => ((!hasHardware(design.shape) && (i === LAST - 2 || i === LAST - 1)) || (i === INSIDE_STEP && noInside(design)) || skipStep(i, design)) ? "" :
    `<button type="button" class="step ${i === ui.step ? "current" : ""} ${ui.visited.has(i) && i !== ui.step ? "done" : ""}" data-step="${i}" ${i === ui.step ? 'aria-current="step"' : ""}>${name}</button>`
  ).join("");
  const cur = els.steps.querySelector(".current");
  if (cur) els.steps.scrollTo({ left: cur.offsetLeft - els.steps.clientWidth / 2 + cur.clientWidth / 2 });
}
function renderPanel() {
  syncBoard(design);
  els.panel.innerHTML = exampleBar() + secTag() + stylesBar() + STEP_VIEWS[ui.step]();
  startCatSlides();
  els.back.hidden = ui.step === 0;
  $("bigview").hidden = ui.step < 3;
  els.next.hidden = ui.step === LAST;
  const bc = $("bar-cart"), bb = $("bar-buy");
  if (bc) { bc.hidden = bb.hidden = ui.step !== LAST; bc.textContent = U("addCart"); bb.textContent = U("buyNow"); }
  els.next.textContent = ui.step === 0 ? U("startDesigning") : ui.step === LAST - 1 ? U("toReview") : U("next");
  els.back.textContent = U("back");
  renderBarSummary();
}
function renderBarSummary() {
  const box = document.getElementById("bar-summary");
  if (!box) return;
  const d = design;
  const tas = d.tassel.photo && d.tassel.type !== "none" ? (CONFIG.tassels.find((x) => x.id === d.tassel.photo) || {}).name : "";
  const dots = d.style !== "ocean" && d.picked && d.picked.length ? `<span class="bs-dots">${d.picked.map((c) => `<i style="background:${c}"></i>`).join("")}</span>` : "";
  box.innerHTML = `<span class="bs-item"><small>${U("bsShape")}</small><b>${esc(shapeName(d.shape))}</b></span>
    <span class="bs-item"><small>${U("bsStyle")}</small><b>${esc(lab("style", d.style))}</b></span>
    ${dots ? `<span class="bs-item"><small>${U("colours")}</small>${dots}</span>` : ""}
    ${tas ? `<span class="bs-item"><small>${U("tassel")}</small><b>${esc(tas)}</b></span>` : ""}`;
}
function renderStatic() {
  applyStaticText();
}
function render() {
  saveAuto();
  curFont = design.font || "block";
  renderStatic();
  renderPreview();
  renderTotal();
  renderSteps();
  renderPanel();
  renderStudioTools();
}
function swing() { /* no flicker: the piece no longer bounces when it changes */ }
// Each style keeps only its own settings, so nothing from the previous style stays on the piece.
function normaliseStyle(d) {
  if (d.ocean && d.ocean.sand === "none") d.ocean.sand = "low";
  if (d.ink) { d.ink.shimmer = "none"; d.ink.cells = false; d.ink.background = "clear"; }
  if (d.style === "ink") { d.ink.cells = false; d.ink.background = "clear"; }
  if (d.style === "ocean") { d.picked = []; d.colors = DEFAULT_COLORS.slice(); d.inkDrops = []; d.crush = []; d.letters.text = ""; d.gradient = { direction: "down", glitter: "none" }; }
  else if (d.style === "clear") { d.picked = []; d.colors = DEFAULT_COLORS.slice(); d.inkDrops = []; }
  else if (d.style === "gradient") { d.inkDrops = []; }
}
function changed() {
  normaliseStyle(design);
  const keepY = window.scrollY;
  setTimeout(() => { if (Math.abs(window.scrollY - keepY) > 2) window.scrollTo(0, keepY); }, 0);
  applyPicked(design);
  setTimeout(renderBarSummary, 0);
  design.paint = [];
  for (const id of Object.keys(design.addins)) { const ad = CONFIG.addins.find((x) => x.id === id); if (!ad || !fits(ad, design)) delete design.addins[id]; }
  if (noInside(design) || design.style === "ocean") design.letters.text = "";
  if (design.style === "ocean") design.crush = [];
  render();
  swing();
}
const LETTER_STEP = 2;
function go(step) {
  if (step === 1 && !ui.cat && !(new URLSearchParams(location.search)).has("start") && design.shape) ui.cat = ui.cat || catOf(design.shape);
  const dir = step >= ui.step ? 1 : -1;
  step = Math.max(0, Math.min(LAST, step));
  if (step === LETTER_STEP && !isLetter(design.shape) && !isNumber(design.shape)) step += dir;
  if (step === INSIDE_STEP && noInside(design)) step += dir;
  while (skipStep(step, design) && step > 0 && step < LAST) step += dir;
  if (design.style === "clear" && (step === 6 || step === 7) && !ui.visited) step = step;
  if (!hasHardware(design.shape) && (step === LAST - 2 || step === LAST - 1)) step = dir > 0 ? LAST : LAST - 3;
  ui.step = Math.max(0, Math.min(LAST, step));
  if (ui.step > 1) ui.chosen = true;
  ui.visited.add(ui.step);
  render();
  const reduce = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
  const top = document.querySelector(".studio").getBoundingClientRect().top + window.scrollY - (window.innerWidth < 900 ? els.stage.offsetHeight : 0) - 8;
  window.scrollTo({ top: Math.max(0, Math.min(window.scrollY, top)), behavior: reduce ? "auto" : "smooth" });
}

let toastTimer;
function toast(msg, actionLabel, action) {
  els.toastText.textContent = msg;
  els.toastAction.hidden = !actionLabel;
  els.toastAction.textContent = actionLabel || "";
  els.toastAction.onclick = action ? () => { action(); els.toast.classList.remove("show"); } : null;
  els.toast.classList.add("show");
  clearTimeout(toastTimer);
  toastTimer = setTimeout(() => els.toast.classList.remove("show"), actionLabel ? 5000 : 2600);
}

/* ---------- Surprise me & Start over ---------- */
function surprise() {
  const before = copy(design);
  const cols = CONFIG.colors.map((c) => c.hex);
  const d = defaultDesign();
  d.shape = design.shape; d.second = design.second; d.note = design.note;
  d.style = pick(["clear", "gradient", "gradient", "ink", "ink", "ocean"]);
  d.picked = Math.random() < 0.7 ? copy(pick(CONFIG.palettes).colors) : [pick(cols), pick(cols), pick(cols)];
  d.colors = d.picked.slice();
  d.gradient = { direction: pick(["down", "right", "diagonal", "center"]), glitter: "none" };
  d.ink = { amount: pick(["light", "medium", "bold"]), background: pick(["clear", "white", "cloudy"]),
    spread: pick(["all", "all", "top", "bottom", "left", "right"]), cells: Math.random() < 0.6, shimmer: "none" };
  d.ocean = { sand: pick(["low", "middle", "middle", "high"]), wave: pick(["top", "middle", "bottom", "diagonal"]),
    foam: pick(["soft", "frothy"]), sandColor: pick(["natural", "natural", "white", "golden", "pink"]) };
  const natural = ["frog", "sea-starfish", "sea-shell-mix1", "sea-shell-mix2", "sea-shell-mix3", "sea-shell-mix4", "sea-shell-scallop", "sea-shell-cone"];
  let pool = CONFIG.addins.filter((a) => a.group !== "charms" && !blockedReason(a.id, d)).sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
  
  pool.slice(0, 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)).forEach((a) => {
    d.addins[a.id] = { amount: a.img ? "few" : pick(["few", "some", "lots"]), place: "anna", color: natural.includes(a.id) ? a.color : pick(cols) };
  });
  d.metal = pick(["gold", "silver", "rose", "black"]);
  d.hardware = pick(["ring", "ringchain", "ringchain", "lobster", "swivel", "carabiner"]);
  d.tassel = { type: pick(["silk", "silk", "cotton", "none"]), ombre: Math.random() < 0.6, color1: pick(cols), color2: pick(cols), cord: pick(["gold", "silver", "match", "twist"]) };
  d.crush = [];
  for (let k = 0; k < 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 2); k++) fillGlass(d, pick(CONFIG.glitters).id, 0.35 + Math.random() * 0.4);
  design = d;
  ui.pourHistory = [];
  ui.side = "front";
  if (ui.step < 3) { ui.step = 3; ui.visited.add(3); }
  changed();
  toast(U("surprised"), U("undo"), () => { design = before; changed(); });
}

function startOver() {
  ui.chosen = false;
  const before = copy(design);
  const step = ui.step;
  design = defaultDesign();
  ui.side = "front";
  ui.visited = new Set([0]);
  go(0);
  toast(U("startedOver"), U("undo"), () => { design = before; go(step); });
}

/* ---------- Panel events ---------- */
function addDesignToCart() {
  const d2 = copy(design); if (d2.crush.length > 500) d2.crush = d2.crush.slice(-500);
  const sub = summaryLines(design, "en").slice(0, 3).map(([k, v]) => v).join(" · ");
  const ok = addToCart({ title: shapeName(design.shape, "en") + " – " + lab("style", design.style, "en"), sub, thumb: renderPiece(d2).replace(/<svg /, '<svg class="cart-svg" '), text: orderText().split("\n").slice(1).join("\n") });
  toast(ok ? U("addedCart") : U("cartFail"));
}
$("bar-cart").addEventListener("click", addDesignToCart);
$("bar-buy").addEventListener("click", () => { copyText(orderText()).catch(() => {}); window.open(igLink(), "_blank", "noopener"); });
els.panel.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
  const gl = e.target.closest("[data-glitter]");
  if (gl) {
    ui.pour.id = ui.pour.id === gl.dataset.glitter ? "" : gl.dataset.glitter;
    renderPanel(); renderHint();
    return;
  }
  const pr = e.target.closest("[data-pour]");
  if (pr) { ui.pour[pr.dataset.pour] = pr.dataset.value; renderPanel(); return; }
  const el = e.target.closest("[data-set], [data-action]");
  if (!el || el.disabled) return;

  if (el.dataset.set && el.tagName === "BUTTON") {
    const path = el.dataset.set;
    setPath(design, path, parseVal(el.dataset.value));
    if (path === "tassel.type") design.tassel.photo = "";
    if (path === "font") { if (design.font === "groovy" && isNumber(design.shape)) design.shape = "A"; resetItems(design); changed(); return; }
    if (path === "shape") {
      resetItems(design);
      if (!design.letters.moved) [design.letters.x, design.letters.y] = bestTextSpot(design.shape);
    }
    if (path === "style" && ui.step === 3) { changed(); setTimeout(() => go(neighbour(1)), 280); return; }
    const m = path.match(/^addins\.(\w+)\.(amount|place)$/);
    if (m) { delete design.addins[m[1]].items; delete design.addins[m[1]].moved; }
    changed();
    return;
  }

  const action = el.dataset.action;
  if (action === "preset") {
    const p = CONFIG.presets.find((x) => x.id === el.dataset.id);
    design = presetDesign(p);
    ui.side = "front";
    go(el.dataset.go === "review" ? LAST : 1);
    swing();
  } else if (action === "gallery") {
    const gd = galleryDesign(el.dataset.id);
    if (gd) { design = gd.d; ui.side = "front"; ui.visited = new Set([0, 1, 2, 3]); ui.chosen = true; go(el.dataset.go === "review" ? LAST : 3); swing(); }
  } else if (action === "tassel") {
    design.tassel.photo = design.tassel.photo === el.dataset.id ? "" : el.dataset.id;
    if (design.tassel.photo && design.tassel.type === "none") design.tassel.type = "silk";
    if (design.tassel.photo && design.hardware === "none") design.hardware = "ringchain";
    changed();
  } else if (action === "addcart") {
    addDesignToCart();
  } else if (action === "exorder") {
    ui.example = false; go(LAST);
  } else if (action === "exedit") {
    ui.example = false; renderPanel();
  } else if (action === "savestyle") {
    saveStyle();
  } else if (action === "opensaves") {
    openSaves();
  } else if (action === "copycode") {
    copyText(shareLink()).then(() => toast(U("linkCopied"))).catch(() => toast(U("linkFail")));
  } else if (action === "draft") {
    saveDraft();
  } else if (action === "inkcol") {
    ui.inkCol = Number(el.dataset.i); renderPanel();
  } else if (action === "inkundo") {
    design.inkDrops.pop(); renderPanel(); renderPreview();
  } else if (action === "inkclear") {
    design.inkDrops = []; renderPanel(); renderPreview();
  } else if (action === "variant") {
    const fk = el.dataset.fam, F = FAMILIES[fk], to = el.dataset.id;
    const have = F.ids.find((id) => design.addins[id]);
    ui.fam[fk] = to;
    if (have && have !== to) { design.addins[to] = Object.assign({}, design.addins[have]); delete design.addins[have]; }
    changed();
  } else if (action === "loaddraft") {
    openSaves();
  } else if (action === "deco") {
    design.deco = el.dataset.id;
    for (const id of Object.keys(design.addins)) { const ad = CONFIG.addins.find((x) => x.id === id); if (!ad || !fits(ad, design)) delete design.addins[id]; }
    renderPanel(); renderPreview(); renderTotal();
  } else if (action === "notassel") {
    design.tassel.photo = ""; design.tassel.type = "none"; changed();
  } else if (action === "photooff") {
    design.photo.on = false; ui.photo = ""; changed();
  } else if (action === "ltshuffle") {
    design.letters.seed = Math.floor(Math.random() * 9999);
    changed();
  } else if (action === "glfill") {
    const n = fillGlass(design, ui.pour.id);
    ui.pourHistory.push(n);
    changed();
  } else if (action === "glundo") {
    const n = ui.pourHistory.pop() || 0;
    design.crush.splice(Math.max(0, design.crush.length - n), n);
    changed();
  } else if (action === "glclear") {
    design.crush = [];
    ui.pourHistory = [];
    changed();
  } else if (action === "cat") {
    ui.cat = el.dataset.id;
    if (ui.cat === "cutting" && catOf(design.shape) !== "cutting") {
      design.shape = "board-rect"; applyDefaults(design); design.style = "clear"; design.addins = {}; design.crush = []; resetItems(design); ui.chosen = true; ui.zoom = 1; ui.pan = [0, 0];
      changed();
    } else renderPanel();
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "auto" });
    return;
  } else if (action === "mold") {
    ui.chosen = true;
    design.shape = el.dataset.shape;
    if (el.dataset.extra) Object.assign(design, JSON.parse(el.dataset.extra));
    applyDefaults(design);
    if (customShape(design.shape) && customShape(design.shape).wood) design.style = "clear";
    if (isGame(design.shape)) design.style = "gradient";
    design.letters.moved = false;
    [design.letters.x, design.letters.y] = bestTextSpot(design.shape);
    resetItems(design);
    ui.zoom = 1; ui.pan = [0, 0];
    changed();
  } else if (action === "scratch") {
    design = defaultDesign();
    go(1);
  } else if (action === "goto") {
    go(Number(el.dataset.step));
  } else if (action === "palette") {
    design.picked = copy(CONFIG.palettes[Number(el.dataset.i)].colors);
    changed();
  } else if (action === "slot") {
    design.picked.splice(Number(el.dataset.i), 1);
    changed();
  } else if (action === "swatch") {
    const at = design.picked.indexOf(el.dataset.hex);
    if (at >= 0) design.picked.splice(at, 1);
    else if (design.picked.length < 3) design.picked.push(el.dataset.hex);
    else { toast(U("maxColours")); return; }
    changed();
  } else if (action === "addin") {
    let a = CONFIG.addins.find((x) => x.id === el.dataset.id);
    { const fk0 = familyOf(a.id); if (fk0 && FAMILIES[fk0].random && !FAMILIES[fk0].ids.some((id) => design.addins[id])) { const F0 = FAMILIES[fk0]; a = CONFIG.addins.find((x) => x.id === F0.ids[Math.floor(Math.random() * F0.ids.length)]); } else if (fk0 && FAMILIES[fk0].random) { a = CONFIG.addins.find((x) => x.id === FAMILIES[fk0].ids.find((id) => design.addins[id])); } }
    if (design.addins[a.id]) delete design.addins[a.id];
    else {
      const [T, B] = bounds(design.shape), n = Object.keys(design.addins).length;
      const taken = Object.keys(design.addins).flatMap((k) => (design.addins[k].items || []).map(([x, y]) => [x, y, itemRadius(k)]));
      const p = LAYOUT[n % LAYOUT.length];
      const spot = placeNear(design, a.id, 40 + p[0] * 220, T + p[1] * (B - T), taken) || placeNear(design, a.id, 150, (T + B) / 2, taken);
      if (!spot) { toast(U("noRoom")); return; }
      design.addins[a.id] = { amount: "few", place: "anna", color: a.color, items: [[spot[0], spot[1], 0]], moved: true };
    }
    changed();
  } else if (action === "shuffle") {
    const a = CONFIG.addins.find((x) => x.id === el.dataset.id);
    const sel = design.addins[a.id];
    sel.items = genItems(a, sel, design, Math.random);
    delete sel.moved;
    renderPreview();
    swing();
  } else if (action === "onemore") {
    const sel = design.addins[el.dataset.id];
    const [x, y] = randomSpot(design, sel.place, Math.random);
    sel.items.push([x, y, Math.round(Math.random() * 40 - 20)]);
    renderPreview();
    swing();
  } else if (action === "dm") {
    copyText(orderText())
      .then(() => toast(U("copied")))
      .catch(() => toast(U("copyFail")));
  } else if (action === "copylink") {
    copyText(shareLink())
      .then(() => toast(U("linkCopied")))
      .catch(() => toast(U("linkFail")));
  }
});

els.panel.addEventListener("input", (e) => {
  const el = e.target;
  if (!el.dataset.set) return;
  let v = el.value;
  if (el.dataset.upper) {
    v = v.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/g, "").slice(0, 1);
    el.value = v;
  }
  if (el.dataset.ltr) {
    v = v.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9 &]/g, "").slice(0, 4);
    if (v !== el.value) el.value = v;
    if (!design.letters.moved) [design.letters.x, design.letters.y] = bestTextSpot(design.shape);
  }
  if (el.dataset.num) v = Number(v);
  setPath(design, el.dataset.set, v);
  renderPreview();
  renderTotal();
  const box = $("order-text");
  if (box) box.value = orderText();
});
els.panel.addEventListener("toggle", (e) => {
  const d = e.target;
  if (!d.classList || !(d.classList.contains("ins-sec") || d.classList.contains("inspo"))) return;
  if (d.open) ui.open.add(d.dataset.key); else ui.open.delete(d.dataset.key);
}, true);
els.panel.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
  if (e.target.type === "color") renderPanel();
  if (e.target.id === "piece-photo" && e.target.files[0]) {
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(e.target.files[0]);
    const im = new Image();
    im.onload = () => { if (ui.photo) URL.revokeObjectURL(ui.photo); ui.photo = url; ui.photoDims = [im.naturalWidth, im.naturalHeight]; design.photo = { on: true, x: 0, y: 0, zoom: 1 }; changed(); };
    im.src = url;
    return;
  }
  if (e.target.id === "photo-input" && e.target.files[0]) {
    if (ui.photo) URL.revokeObjectURL(ui.photo);
    ui.photo = URL.createObjectURL(e.target.files[0]);
    renderPanel();
  }
});

/* ---------- Drag items & paint on the preview ---------- */
let gesture = null;

function svgPoint(e) {
  const svg = els.preview.querySelector("svg");
  const pt = svg.createSVGPoint();
  pt.x = e.clientX; pt.y = e.clientY;
  return pt.matrixTransform(svg.getScreenCTM().inverse());
}

els.preview.addEventListener("pointerdown", (e) => {
  if (ui.side !== "front") return;
  const p = svgPoint(e);
  if (inking()) {
    if (!insideShape(design.shape, p.x, p.y)) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    addInk(p.x, p.y);
    renderPanel();
    return;
  }
  if (pouring()) {
    if (!nearShape(design.shape, p.x, p.y)) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    gesture = { type: "pour", lx: p.x, ly: p.y, n: 0, g: els.preview.querySelector("g.crush") };
    gesture.n += addStamp(gesture, p.x, p.y);
    els.preview.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    return;
  }
  if (false) {
    if (!insideShape(design.shape, p.x, p.y)) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    const live = els.preview.querySelector(".live");
    const path = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "path");
    path.setAttribute("fill", "none");
    path.setAttribute("stroke", brushColor());
    path.setAttribute("stroke-width", BRUSH[ui.brush.size]);
    path.setAttribute("stroke-linecap", "round");
    path.setAttribute("stroke-linejoin", "round");
    path.setAttribute("opacity", ui.brush.tool === "ink" ? "0.85" : "0.6");
    if (ui.brush.tool !== "ink") path.setAttribute("stroke-dasharray", "1 6");
    live.appendChild(path);
    gesture = { type: "paint", pts: [Math.round(p.x), Math.round(p.y)], path };
    path.setAttribute("d", strokePath(gesture.pts));
    els.preview.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    return;
  }
  const ltr = e.target.closest(".ltr");
  if (ltr && design.letters.text) {
    e.preventDefault();
    gesture = { type: "ltr", el: ltr, sx: p.x, sy: p.y, ox: design.letters.x, oy: design.letters.y };
    els.preview.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    return;
  }
  const item = e.target.closest(".item");
  if (!item) {
    if (ui.zoom <= 1 && Object.values(design.addins).some((s) => s.items && s.items.length)) { e.preventDefault(); marqueeStart(e); return; }
    if (ui.zoom > 1) {
      e.preventDefault();
      gesture = { type: "pan", sx: e.clientX, sy: e.clientY, ox: ui.pan[0], oy: ui.pan[1] };
      els.preview.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    }
    return;
  }
  e.preventDefault();
  const id = item.dataset.id, i = Number(item.dataset.i);
  const it = design.addins[id].items[i];
  if (e.shiftKey) { if (!ui.sels.some((q) => q.id === id && q.i === i)) ui.sels.push({ id, i }); } else if (!ui.sels.some((q) => q.id === id && q.i === i)) ui.sels = [{ id, i }];
  markSelected();
  gesture = { type: "drag", id, i, el: item, sx: p.x, sy: p.y, ox: it[0], oy: it[1] };
  item.classList.add("dragging");
  els.preview.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
});

els.preview.addEventListener("pointermove", (e) => {
  if (!gesture) return;
  const p = svgPoint(e);
  if (gesture.type === "ltr") {
    const p = svgPoint(e), L = design.letters;
    L.x = Math.round(Math.max(20, Math.min(280, gesture.ox + p.x - gesture.sx)));
    L.y = Math.round(Math.max(120, Math.min(420, gesture.oy + p.y - gesture.sy)));
    gesture.el.setAttribute("transform", `translate(${L.x} ${L.y}) rotate(${L.layout === "scatter" ? 0 : L.rot})`);
    if (L.layout === "scatter") renderPreview();
    return;
  }
  if (gesture.type === "ink") {
    const p = svgPoint(e);
    if (Math.hypot(p.x - gesture.lx, p.y - gesture.ly) < 14) return;
    gesture.lx = p.x; gesture.ly = p.y;
    if (insideShape(design.shape, p.x, p.y)) addInk(p.x, p.y);
    return;
  }
  if (gesture.type === "pour") {
    const p = svgPoint(e);
    if (Math.hypot(p.x - gesture.lx, p.y - gesture.ly) < Math.max(4, GLASS_RADIUS[ui.pour.size] * 0.7)) return;
    gesture.lx = p.x; gesture.ly = p.y;
    if (nearShape(design.shape, p.x, p.y)) gesture.n += addStamp(gesture, p.x, p.y);
    return;
  }
  if (gesture.type === "marquee") { marqueeMove(e); return; }
  if (gesture.type === "pan") {
    const svg = els.preview.querySelector("svg");
    const k = (300 / ui.zoom) / svg.getBoundingClientRect().width;
    ui.pan = [gesture.ox - (e.clientX - gesture.sx) * k, gesture.oy - (e.clientY - gesture.sy) * k];
    svg.setAttribute("viewBox", zoomBox(ui.zoom, ui.pan));
    return;
  }
  if (gesture.type === "drag") {
    const it = design.addins[gesture.id].items[gesture.i];
    it[0] = Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(300, gesture.ox + p.x - gesture.sx)));
    it[1] = Math.round(Math.max(100, Math.min(440, gesture.oy + p.y - gesture.sy)));
    gesture.el.setAttribute("transform", `translate(${it[0]} ${it[1]}) rotate(${it[2]})`);
    gesture.el.classList.toggle("leaving", !insideShape(design.shape, it[0], it[1]));
  } else {
    const pts = gesture.pts;
    const lx = pts[pts.length - 2], ly = pts[pts.length - 1];
    if (Math.hypot(p.x - lx, p.y - ly) < 3) return;
    pts.push(Math.round(p.x), Math.round(p.y));
    gesture.path.setAttribute("d", strokePath(pts));
  }
});

let inkRaf = 0;
function addInk(x, y) {
  design.inkDrops.push([Math.min(ui.inkCol, design.picked.length - 1), Math.round(x), Math.round(y), { s: 12, m: 20, l: 32 }[design.inkSize]]);
  if (design.inkDrops.length > 160) design.inkDrops.shift();
  cancelAnimationFrame(inkRaf); inkRaf = requestAnimationFrame(renderPreview);
}
function endGesture() {
  if (!gesture) return;
  const g = gesture;
  gesture = null;
  if (g.type === "marquee") { g.box.remove(); return; }
  if (g.type === "pan") return;
  if (g.type === "ltr") { design.letters.moved = true; const box = $("order-text"); if (box) box.value = orderText(); return; }
  if (g.type === "ink") { renderPanel(); const box = $("order-text"); if (box) box.value = orderText(); return; }
  if (g.type === "pour") {
    ui.pourHistory.push(g.n);
    renderPanel();
    renderStudioTools();
    const box = $("order-text");
    if (box) box.value = orderText();
    return;
  }
  if (g.type === "drag") {
    const sel = design.addins[g.id];
    const it = sel.items[g.i];
    if (!itemFits(design.shape, it[0], it[1], g.id)) {
      sel.items.splice(g.i, 1);
      if (!sel.items.length) delete design.addins[g.id];
      toast(U("removed"));
      render();
    } else {
      sel.moved = true;
      g.el.classList.remove("dragging");
      renderTotal();
    }
  } else {
    if (design.paint.length >= 40) design.paint.shift();
    design.paint.push({ tool: ui.brush.tool, color: brushColor(), size: ui.brush.size, pts: g.pts });
    renderPreview();
    renderTotal();
    renderStudioTools();
  }
  const box = $("order-text");
  if (box) box.value = orderText();
}
els.preview.addEventListener("pointerup", endGesture);
els.preview.addEventListener("pointercancel", endGesture);

// draw new chips straight into the picture while the finger moves
function addStamp(g, x, y) {
  const before = design.crush.length;
  const add = stamp(design, ui.pour.id, Math.round(x), Math.round(y));
  if (g.g) g.g.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", add.map(glassSprite).join(""));
  const over = before + add.length - design.crush.length;   // oldest chips that were dropped
  for (let i = 0; i < over && g.g && g.g.firstChild; i++) g.g.removeChild(g.g.firstChild);
  return add.length;
}

/* ---------- Zoom (buttons, slider, ctrl+wheel) ---------- */
function setZoom(z) {
  ui.zoom = Math.max(1, Math.min(4, Math.round(z * 4) / 4));
  if (ui.zoom === 1) ui.pan = [0, 0];
  renderPreview();
  renderStudioTools();
}
$("zoomin").addEventListener("click", () => setZoom(ui.zoom + 0.5));
$("zoomout").addEventListener("click", () => setZoom(ui.zoom - 0.5));
els.preview.addEventListener("wheel", (e) => {
  if (!e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !ui.full) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  setZoom(ui.zoom + (e.deltaY < 0 ? 0.25 : -0.25));
}, { passive: false });

/* ---------- Drag an item from the list onto the keychain (like Canva) ---------- */
let tray = null, suppressClick = false;
els.panel.addEventListener("pointerdown", (e) => {
  const h = e.target.closest("[data-drag], [data-gdrag]");
  if (!h || e.button > 0) return;
  tray = { id: h.dataset.drag || h.dataset.gdrag, glass: !!h.dataset.gdrag, x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, ghost: null, pid: e.pointerId };
  h.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
});
els.panel.addEventListener("pointermove", (e) => {
  if (!tray) return;
  if (!tray.ghost) {
    if (Math.hypot(e.clientX - tray.x, e.clientY - tray.y) < 6) return;
    const a = tray.glass ? null : CONFIG.addins.find((x) => x.id === tray.id);
    tray.ghost = document.createElement("div");
    tray.ghost.className = "drag-ghost";
    if (tray.glass) tray.ghost.style.background = `url(images/glitter/${tray.id}.png) center/cover, #3E8E99`;
    else tray.ghost.style.background = a.color;
    document.body.appendChild(tray.ghost);
    els.preview.classList.add("drop-target");
  }
  tray.ghost.style.left = e.clientX + "px";
  tray.ghost.style.top = e.clientY + "px";
});
function endTray(e, cancel) {
  if (!tray) return;
  const t0 = tray;
  tray = null;
  if (!t0.ghost) return;
  t0.ghost.remove();
  els.preview.classList.remove("drop-target");
  suppressClick = true;
  setTimeout(() => (suppressClick = false), 0);
  if (cancel) return;
  const svg = els.preview.querySelector("svg");
  const r = svg.getBoundingClientRect();
  if (e.clientX < r.left || e.clientX > r.right || e.clientY < r.top || e.clientY > r.bottom) return;
  const p = svgPoint(e);
  if (!nearShape(design.shape, p.x, p.y)) { toast(U("dropInside")); return; }
  if (t0.glass) {
    const n = stamp(design, t0.id, Math.round(p.x), Math.round(p.y), "l", ui.pour.size).length;
    ui.pourHistory.push(n);
    changed();
    return;
  }
  if (!itemFits(design.shape, p.x, p.y, t0.id)) { toast(U("dropInside")); return; }
  dropAddin(t0.id, Math.round(p.x), Math.round(p.y));
}
els.panel.addEventListener("pointerup", (e) => endTray(e, false));
els.panel.addEventListener("pointercancel", (e) => endTray(e, true));
els.panel.addEventListener("click", (e) => { if (suppressClick) { e.stopPropagation(); e.preventDefault(); } }, true);

function dropAddin(id, x, y) {
  const a = CONFIG.addins.find((v) => v.id === id);
  if (blockedReason(id, design)) { toast(blockedReason(id, design)); return; }
  const [T, B] = bounds(design.shape);
  const place = y < T + (B - T) * 0.38 ? "top" : y > T + (B - T) * 0.62 ? "bottom" : "middle";
  if (!design.addins[id]) design.addins[id] = { amount: "few", place, color: a.color };
  const sel = design.addins[id];
  if (a.drag) {
    if (!Array.isArray(sel.items)) sel.items = [];
    if (a.single) sel.items = [];
    sel.items.push([x, y, a.id === "frog" ? 0 : Math.round(Math.random() * 40 - 20)]);
    sel.moved = true;
  } else {
    sel.place = place;
  }
  changed();
}

/* ---------- Big view ---------- */
function setFull(on) {
  ui.full = on;
  els.stage.classList.toggle("full", on);
  document.body.classList.toggle("locked", on);
  renderPreview();
  renderStudioTools();
  if (on) $("done").focus();
}
els.studioTools.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
  const gb = e.target.closest("[data-glitter], [data-pour], [data-action]");
  if (gb && !gb.disabled) {
    if (gb.dataset.glitter) { ui.pour.id = ui.pour.id === gb.dataset.glitter ? "" : gb.dataset.glitter; }
    else if (gb.dataset.pour) { ui.pour[gb.dataset.pour] = gb.dataset.value; }
    else if (gb.dataset.action === "glfill" && ui.pour.id) { ui.pourHistory.push(fillGlass(design, ui.pour.id)); }
    else if (gb.dataset.action === "glundo") { const n = ui.pourHistory.pop() || 0; design.crush.splice(Math.max(0, design.crush.length - n), n); }
    else if (gb.dataset.action === "glclear") { design.crush = []; ui.pourHistory = []; }
    renderPreview(); renderStudioTools(); renderPanel(); e.stopPropagation(); return;
  }
  const b = e.target.closest("[data-tool]");
  if (!b || b.disabled || b.type === "range") return;
  const tool = b.dataset.tool, v = b.dataset.value;
  if (tool === "mode") ui.mode = v;
  else if (tool === "brush") { ui.brush.tool = v; ui.brush.color = ""; }
  else if (tool === "size") ui.brush.size = v;
  else if (tool === "color") ui.brush.color = v;
  else if (tool === "undo") { design.paint.pop(); renderTotal(); }
  else if (tool === "clear") { design.paint = []; renderTotal(); }
  renderPreview();
  renderStudioTools();
});
els.studioTools.addEventListener("input", (e) => {
  if (e.target.dataset.tool === "zoom") {
    ui.zoom = Number(e.target.value);
    if (ui.zoom === 1) ui.pan = [0, 0];
    renderPreview();
  }
});
$("bigview").addEventListener("click", () => setFull(true));
$("done").addEventListener("click", () => { setFull(false); renderPanel(); });
document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => { if (e.key === "Escape" && ui.full) { setFull(false); renderPanel(); } });
$("bigview").addEventListener("dblclick", (e) => e.preventDefault());
document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
  if (e.key === "Escape" && ui.full) { setFull(false); renderPanel(); }
});

/* ---------- Other buttons ---------- */
els.steps.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
  const b = e.target.closest("[data-step]");
  if (b) go(Number(b.dataset.step));
});
// Clear resin and ink dye: things inside (charms, flowers) come before glitter and sprinkles.
const stepOrder = () => (design.style === "clear" ? [0, 1, 2, 3, 7, 6, 8, 9, 10] : design.style === "ink" ? [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, 6, 8, 9, 10] : null);
function neighbour(dir) {
  const o = stepOrder();
  if (!o) return ui.step + dir;
  const k = o.indexOf(ui.step);
  return k < 0 ? ui.step + dir : o[Math.max(0, Math.min(o.length - 1, k + dir))];
}
els.next.addEventListener("click", () => go(neighbour(1)));
els.back.addEventListener("click", () => go(neighbour(-1)));
$("startover").addEventListener("click", startOver);
$("savedraft").addEventListener("click", saveDraft);
$("mysaves").addEventListener("click", openSaves);
$("savestyle").addEventListener("click", saveStyle);
onLangChange(render);

/* ---------- Open a shared design link (…#d=...) ---------- */
function loadFromLink() {
  const preset = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get("preset");
  const p = CONFIG.presets.find((x) => x.id === preset);
  if (p) {
    design = presetDesign(p); ui.example = true;
    ui.step = 1;
    ui.visited = new Set([0, 1]);
  }
  const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
  const gk = params.get("gallery"), gd = gk && galleryDesign(gk);
  if (gd) { ui.example = true; design = gd.d; ui.cat = gd.cat; ui.step = 3; ui.visited = new Set([0, 1, 2, 3]); }
  if (params.get("cat")) { ui.cat = params.get("cat"); ui.step = 1; ui.visited = new Set([0, 1]); ui.jump = params.get("group") || ""; }
  if (ui.cat === "cutting" && catOf(design.shape) !== "cutting") { design.shape = "board-rect"; design.style = "clear"; design.addins = {}; design.crush = []; ui.chosen = true; }
  if (params.has("start")) { ui.step = 1; ui.visited = new Set([0, 1]); ui.cat = ""; }
  const letter = (params.get("letter") || "").toUpperCase();
  if (letter) ui.cat = "keychains";
  if (/^[A-Z0-9]$/.test(letter)) { design.shape = letter; resetItems(design); ui.step = 2; ui.visited = new Set([0, 1, 2]); }
  if (p || gd || /^[A-Z0-9]$/.test(letter)) ui.chosen = true;
  const m = location.hash.match(/d=([^&]+)/);
  if (!m) return;
  try {
    design = merge(defaultDesign(), decodeDesign(decodeURIComponent(m[1]))); if (!design.picked.length && !isFresh(design)) design.picked = [...new Set(design.colors)];
    ui.step = LAST; ui.chosen = true;
    ui.visited = new Set([...Array(STEP_COUNT).keys()]);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn("Could not read shared design", err);
  }
}

loadFromLink();
renderChrome();
render();
if (new URLSearchParams(location.search).has("saves")) setTimeout(openSaves, 300);
if (ui.jump) setTimeout(() => { const el = document.getElementById("grp-" + ui.jump); if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" }); }, 250);
if (document.fonts && document.fonts.load) {
  Promise.all([document.fonts.load("20px 'Archivo Black'"), document.fonts.load("20px 'Bagel Fat One'")]).catch(() => {}).then(() => { maskCache = {}; render(); });
}
if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) {
  document.fonts.ready.then(() => { maskCache = {}; render(); });
}

/* board size: type or drag */
els.panel.addEventListener("input", (e) => {
  const el = e.target.closest("[data-cm]");
  if (!el) return;
  const k = el.dataset.cm, v = Math.max(10, Math.min(200, Math.round(Number(el.value) || 0)));
  if (!design.cm) design.cm = { w: 30, h: 40 };
  if (el.type === "number" && (el.value === "" || Number(el.value) < 10)) return;   // let the person finish typing
  design.cm[k] = v;
  els.panel.querySelectorAll(`[data-cm="${k}"]`).forEach((x) => { if (x !== el) x.value = v; });
  renderPreview(); renderBarSummary();
  const box = $("order-text"); if (box) box.value = orderText();
});
els.panel.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
  const b = e.target.closest("[data-cm-set]");
  if (!b) return;
  const [w, h] = b.dataset.cmSet.split("x").map(Number);
  design.cm = { w, h };
  renderPanel(); renderPreview(); renderBarSummary();
});

els.panel.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
  const b = e.target.closest("[data-chess]");
  if (!b) return;
  const [sec, key] = b.dataset.chess.split(".");
  if (!design.chess) design.chess = defaultDesign().chess;
  design.chess[sec][key] = key === "foil" ? !!b.dataset.v : b.dataset.v;
  renderPanel(); renderPreview(); renderBarSummary();
  const box = $("order-text"); if (box) box.value = orderText();
});


===== FILE: canvas.js =====
"use strict";

/* =========================================================
   Canvas page (canvas.html): any size, beach style, or your own
   photo cropped into a shape. Drag the photo, zoom, then send by DM.
   ========================================================= */
Object.assign(I18N.en, {
  canvas: {
    backSections: "← Back to sections",
    title: "Design a canvas",
    sub: "Any size. Choose a look from our real pours, or add your own photo and choose the shape it is cut into.",
    kind: "What kind of canvas?", beach: "Sea style", abstract: "Abstract", photo: "Custom gift (my photo)", board: "Wooden cutting board", shapePhoto: "Shape or inspiration picture (optional)", shapeNote: "Type the size above, or upload a picture of the shape you want. Attach the same picture in the Instagram chat.",  colours: "Colours", shuffle: "Different crop", lookLabel: "Choose a look", lookNote: "These are photos of canvases Anna poured. Yours is poured by hand, so it will be similar but never identical. Tell us your colours in the note.", pickSlot: "Colour {n}",
    size: "Size (cm)", width: "Width", height: "Height", presets: "Quick sizes",
    shape: "Cut-out shape", shapes: { circle: "Circle", rect: "Rectangle", rounded: "Rounded", oval: "Oval", heart: "Heart", arch: "Arch", star: "Star" },
    photoLabel: "Your photo", choose: "Choose a photo", change: "Change photo",
    zoom: "Zoom", dragHint: "Drag the photo to move it. Use the slider or scroll to zoom.",
    empty: "Choose a photo to see it here",
    note: "Anything else?", notePh: "Colours, a name, a date, the beach you want…",
    name: "Your name", send: "Send by DM", copied: "Copied! Paste it in the DM and attach your photo.",
    copyFail: "Copy the message below and paste it in the DM.", message: "Your message",
    decor: "Paper flowers and trees", decorHint: "Tap one to add it, then drag it on the picture. Real 3D paper flowers and miniature trees.", flSize: "Flower size", flRemove: "Remove flower",
    boardNote: "Resin on a wooden cutting board, any size, any shape. Tell us the colours and mood you want.",
    beachNote: "Sea style canvases are poured by hand. Pick the look closest to what you want.", abstractNote: "Abstract canvases are poured by hand, so every one is unique. Pick the look closest to what you want.",
  },
});
const C = (k, v) => t("canvas." + k, v);

const SIZES = [[20, 20], [20, 30], [30, 30], [30, 40], [40, 50], [50, 70]];
const SHAPE_IDS = ["circle", "rect", "rounded", "oval", "heart", "arch", "star"];
const cv = { kind: "photo", w: 30, h: 40, shape: "rounded", photo: "", img: null, x: 0, y: 0, zoom: 1, note: "", name: "", decor: [], sel: -1, shapePhoto: "", seed: 3, look: 0 };
const q = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
if (["beach", "abstract", "photo", "board"].includes(q.get("kind"))) cv.kind = q.get("kind");
const OCCASION = (q.get("occasion") || "").slice(0, 40);

function rnd(seed) { let s = seed * 9301 + 49297; return () => ((s = (s * 9301 + 49297) % 233280) / 233280); }
// generated pour: sea (bands, foam wave, sand) or abstract (swirls)
const LOOKS = {
  beach: [["canvas-beach", "Wave and sand"], ["canvas-sea", "Deep sea"], ["sea", "Ocean swirls"]],
  abstract: [["abs-purple", "Purple and gold"], ["abs-green", "Green and gold"], ["abs-plum", "Plum and flowers"], ["abs-copper", "Copper and pearl"]],
};
const ART = {};
function look() { const L = LOOKS[cv.kind]; return L[Math.min(cv.look || 0, L.length - 1)]; }
// real photo of Anna's pour, cropped to the canvas; "New crop" moves the crop
function artSvg(W, H) {
  const id = look()[0], R = rnd(cv.seed), im = ART[id];
  const base = `<rect width="${W}" height="${H}" fill="#223"/>`;
  if (!im) { const i = new Image(); i.onload = () => { ART[id] = i; renderStage(); }; i.src = `images/real/${id}.jpg`; return base; }
  const k = Math.max(W / im.naturalWidth, H / im.naturalHeight) * (1 + R() * 0.25);
  const w = im.naturalWidth * k, h = im.naturalHeight * k;
  return base + `<image href="images/real/${id}.jpg" x="${(-(w - W) * R()).toFixed(1)}" y="${(-(h - H) * R()).toFixed(1)}" width="${w.toFixed(1)}" height="${h.toFixed(1)}" preserveAspectRatio="none"/>`;
}

/* path of the cut-out inside a W x H canvas (with a margin) */
function framePath(shape, W, H) {
  const m = Math.min(W, H) * 0.08;
  const x = m, y = m, w = W - 2 * m, h = H - 2 * m, cx = W / 2, cy = H / 2;
  switch (shape) {
    case "rect": return `M${x} ${y}H${x + w}V${y + h}H${x}Z`;
    case "circle": { const r = Math.min(w, h) / 2; return `M${cx - r} ${cy}A${r} ${r} 0 1 1 ${cx + r} ${cy}A${r} ${r} 0 1 1 ${cx - r} ${cy}Z`; }
    case "oval": return `M${x} ${cy}A${w / 2} ${h / 2} 0 1 1 ${x + w} ${cy}A${w / 2} ${h / 2} 0 1 1 ${x} ${cy}Z`;
    case "arch": return `M${x} ${y + h}V${y + w / 2}A${w / 2} ${w / 2} 0 0 1 ${x + w} ${y + w / 2}V${y + h}Z`;
    case "heart": {
      const s = Math.min(w, h);
      const ox = cx - s / 2, oy = cy - s / 2;
      const P = (a, b) => `${(ox + (a / 100) * s).toFixed(1)} ${(oy + (b / 100) * s).toFixed(1)}`;
      return `M${P(50, 92)}C${P(10, 62)} ${P(2, 34)} ${P(14, 20)}C${P(28, 4)} ${P(46, 12)} ${P(50, 28)}C${P(54, 12)} ${P(72, 4)} ${P(86, 20)}C${P(98, 34)} ${P(90, 62)} ${P(50, 92)}Z`;
    }
    case "star": {
      const R = Math.min(w, h) / 2, r = R * 0.45;
      let d = "";
      for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        const a = (Math.PI / 5) * i - Math.PI / 2, rad = i % 2 ? r : R;
        d += (i ? "L" : "M") + (cx + rad * Math.cos(a)).toFixed(1) + " " + (cy + rad * Math.sin(a)).toFixed(1);
      }
      return d + "Z";
    }
    default: { // rounded
      const r = Math.min(w, h) * 0.12;
      return `M${x + r} ${y}H${x + w - r}Q${x + w} ${y} ${x + w} ${y + r}V${y + h - r}Q${x + w} ${y + h} ${x + w - r} ${y + h}H${x + r}Q${x} ${y + h} ${x} ${y + h - r}V${y + r}Q${x} ${y} ${x + r} ${y}Z`;
    }
  }
}

function dims() {
  const k = 400 / Math.max(cv.w, cv.h);
  return [Math.round(cv.w * k), Math.round(cv.h * k)];
}

/* base "cover" size of the photo for the frame; zoom multiplies it */
function photoBox(W, H) {
  const im = cv.img;
  const m = Math.min(W, H) * 0.08, fw = W - 2 * m, fh = H - 2 * m;
  const base = Math.max(fw / im.naturalWidth, fh / im.naturalHeight);
  const pw = im.naturalWidth * base * cv.zoom, ph = im.naturalHeight * base * cv.zoom;
  return { pw, ph, x: W / 2 - pw / 2 + cv.x, y: H / 2 - ph / 2 + cv.y };
}

function renderStage() {
  const [W, H] = dims();
  const path = framePath(cv.shape, W, H);
  let inner;
  if (cv.kind === "beach" || cv.kind === "abstract") inner = artSvg(W, H);
  else if (cv.img) {
    const b = photoBox(W, H);
    inner = `<image href="${esc(cv.photo)}" x="${b.x.toFixed(1)}" y="${b.y.toFixed(1)}" width="${b.pw.toFixed(1)}" height="${b.ph.toFixed(1)}" preserveAspectRatio="none"/>`;
  } else {
    inner = `<rect width="${W}" height="${H}" fill="#E7EEF0"/><text x="${W / 2}" y="${H / 2}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="14" fill="#6B7780" font-family="Jost, sans-serif">${esc(C("empty"))}</text>`;
  }
  const dec = cv.decor.map((f, i) => `<image class="decor ${i === cv.sel ? "sel" : ""}" data-i="${i}" href="${CONFIG.paperFlowers.find((x) => x.id === f.id).img || "images/paperflowers/" + f.id + ".png"}" x="${f.x - f.s / 2}" y="${f.y - f.s / 2}" width="${f.s}" height="${f.s}" style="filter:drop-shadow(0 2px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.35));cursor:move"/>`).join("");
  document.getElementById("cv-stage").innerHTML = `<div class="sz-wrap" id="sz-wrap"><span class="sz-label">${Math.round(cv.w)} × ${Math.round(cv.h)} cm</span><i class="sz-grip r" data-grip="r"></i><i class="sz-grip b" data-grip="b"></i><i class="sz-grip c" data-grip="c"></i>
    <svg viewBox="0 0 ${W} ${H}" role="img" aria-label="${esc(C("title"))}">
      <defs><clipPath id="cv-clip"><path d="${path}"/></clipPath></defs>
      <rect width="${W}" height="${H}" fill="${cv.kind === "board" ? "#C8975C" : "#fff"}"/>
      <g clip-path="url(#cv-clip)">${inner}</g>
      <path d="${path}" fill="none" stroke="rgba(0,0,0,0.25)" stroke-width="1"/>
      ${dec}
    </svg></div>`;
}

// update the photo in place (rebuilding the SVG would drop the drag)
function moveImage() {
  const el = document.querySelector("#cv-stage image");
  if (!el || !cv.img) return;
  const [W, H] = dims();
  const b = photoBox(W, H);
  el.setAttribute("x", b.x.toFixed(1)); el.setAttribute("y", b.y.toFixed(1));
  el.setAttribute("width", b.pw.toFixed(1)); el.setAttribute("height", b.ph.toFixed(1));
}

function cvText() {
  const lines = ["Hi Anna! I'd like a canvas.", "", `Kind: ${{ abstract: "Abstract", beach: "Sea style", photo: "Custom gift (my photo)", board: "Wooden cutting board" }[cv.kind]}`, `Size: ${cv.w} x ${cv.h} cm`];
  if (OCCASION) lines.splice(3, 0, `Occasion: ${OCCASION}`);
  if (cv.shapePhoto) lines.push("(I'll attach a picture of the shape I want)");
  if (cv.kind === "beach" || cv.kind === "abstract") lines.push("Look: " + look()[1] + " (like the photo in the app)");
  if (cv.kind === "photo") lines.push(`Cut-out shape: ${t("canvas.shapes." + cv.shape, null, "en")}`, cv.photo ? "(I'll attach my photo in this chat)" : "(photo to follow)");
  if (cv.decor.length) lines.push("Paper flowers: " + cv.decor.map((f) => CONFIG.paperFlowers.find((x) => x.id === f.id).name).join(", "));
  if (cv.note.trim()) lines.push("Note: " + cv.note.trim());
  if (cv.name.trim()) lines.push("Name: " + cv.name.trim());
  if (ui.lang !== "en") lines.push("Language: " + { th: "Thai", ru: "Russian" }[ui.lang]);
  return lines.join("\n");
}

function renderCanvas() {
  const root = document.getElementById("canvas");
  if (!root) return;
  const chip = (on, attrs, label) => `<button type="button" class="chip ${on ? "on" : ""}" aria-pressed="${on}" ${attrs}>${label}</button>`;
  root.innerHTML = `
    <div class="canvas-page">
      <div class="cv-stage" id="cv-stage"></div>
      <div class="cv-panel">
        <div><a class="btn btn-line small" href="design.html?start=1" style="margin-bottom:14px">${C("backSections")}</a><h1>${C("title")}</h1><p class="lead">${C("sub")}</p></div>
        <div class="field"><span class="field-label">${C("kind")}</span><div class="chips">
          ${chip(cv.kind === "beach", 'data-kind="beach"', C("beach"))}${chip(cv.kind === "abstract", 'data-kind="abstract"', C("abstract"))}${chip(cv.kind === "photo", 'data-kind="photo"', C("photo"))}${chip(cv.kind === "board", 'data-kind="board"', C("board"))}</div>
          ${OCCASION ? `<p class="small-note"><strong>${OCCASION.replace(/[<>&"]/g, "")}</strong></p>` : ""}${cv.kind === "board" ? `<p class="small-note">${C("boardNote")}</p>` : ""}
          ${cv.kind === "beach" ? `<p class="small-note">${C("beachNote")}</p>` : ""}${cv.kind === "abstract" ? `<p class="small-note">${C("abstractNote")}</p>` : ""}</div>
        <div class="field"><span class="field-label">${C("size")}</span>
          <div class="cv-size">
            <label>${C("width")} <input type="number" min="5" max="200" step="1" id="cv-w" value="${cv.w}"></label> ×
            <label>${C("height")} <input type="number" min="5" max="200" step="1" id="cv-h" value="${cv.h}"></label>
          </div>
          <div class="chips" style="margin-top:10px">${SIZES.map(([w, h]) => chip(cv.w === w && cv.h === h, `data-size="${w}x${h}"`, `${w}×${h}`)).join("")}</div></div>
        ${cv.kind === "beach" || cv.kind === "abstract" ? `<div class="field"><span class="field-label">${C("lookLabel")}</span>
          <div class="gl-grid">${LOOKS[cv.kind].map(([id, nm], i) => `<button type="button" class="gl ${cv.look === i ? "on" : ""}" data-look="${i}"><span class="gl-img"><img src="images/real/${id}.jpg" alt="" style="object-fit:cover"></span><span class="gl-name">${nm}</span></button>`).join("")}</div>
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-line small" style="margin-top:10px" data-shuffle="1">${C("shuffle")}</button>
          <p class="small-note">${C("lookNote")}</p></div>` : ""}
        ${cv.kind !== "photo" ? `<div class="field"><span class="field-label">${C("shapePhoto")}</span><input type="file" accept="image/*" id="cv-shape">${cv.shapePhoto ? `<img src="${cv.shapePhoto}" alt="" style="max-height:120px;margin-top:8px">` : ""}<p class="small-note">${C("shapeNote")}</p></div>` : ""}
        ${cv.kind === "photo" ? `
        <div class="field"><span class="field-label">${C("shape")}</span><div class="chips">
          ${SHAPE_IDS.map((s) => chip(cv.shape === s, `data-shape="${s}"`, C("shapes." + s))).join("")}</div></div>
        <div class="field"><span class="field-label">${C("photoLabel")}</span>
          <input type="file" accept="image/*" id="cv-file">
          ${cv.img ? `<div class="cv-zoom" style="margin-top:12px"><span>${C("zoom")}</span><input type="range" min="1" max="4" step="0.05" value="${cv.zoom}" id="cv-zoom" aria-label="${C("zoom")}"></div><p class="small-note">${C("dragHint")}</p>` : ""}</div>` : ""}
        <div class="field"><span class="field-label">${C("decor")}</span>
          <div class="gl-grid">${CONFIG.paperFlowers.map((f) => `<button type="button" class="gl" data-addflower="${f.id}" title="${esc(f.name)}"><span class="gl-img"><img src="${f.img || "images/paperflowers/" + f.id + ".png"}" alt=""></span><span class="gl-name">${f.name.replace(" paper", "")}</span></button>`).join("")}</div>
          <p class="small-note">${C("decorHint")}</p>
          ${cv.sel >= 0 && cv.decor[cv.sel] ? `<div class="cv-zoom"><span>${C("flSize")}</span><input type="range" min="30" max="260" step="2" value="${cv.decor[cv.sel].s}" id="cv-fsize" aria-label="${C("flSize")}"><button type="button" class="btn btn-line small" data-delflower="1">${C("flRemove")}</button></div>` : ""}</div>
        <div class="field"><span class="field-label">${C("name")}</span><input type="text" class="text-input" style="width:100%;font-size:1rem;text-transform:none" id="cv-name" value="${esc(cv.name)}"></div>
        <div class="field"><span class="field-label">${C("note")}</span><textarea id="cv-note" placeholder="${esc(C("notePh"))}">${esc(cv.note)}</textarea></div>
        <div class="field"><a class="btn btn-ink wide" id="cv-send" href="${igLink()}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${C("send")}</a></div>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  renderStage();
}

const cvToast = (msg) => {
  document.getElementById("toast-text").textContent = msg;
  const el = document.getElementById("toast");
  el.classList.add("show");
  setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove("show"), 2800);
};

document.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
  const sl = e.target.closest("[data-slot]");
  if (sl) { cv.slot = Number(sl.dataset.slot); renderCanvas(); return; }
  const lk = e.target.closest("[data-look]");
  if (lk) { cv.look = Number(lk.dataset.look); renderCanvas(); return; }
  if (e.target.closest("[data-shuffle]")) { cv.seed = Math.floor(Math.random() * 9999) + 1; renderCanvas(); return; }
  const af = e.target.closest("[data-addflower]");
  if (af) { const [W, H] = dims(); cv.decor.push({ id: af.dataset.addflower, x: Math.round(W / 2), y: Math.round(H / 2), s: Math.round(Math.min(W, H) * 0.3) }); cv.sel = cv.decor.length - 1; renderCanvas(); return; }
  if (e.target.closest("[data-delflower]")) { cv.decor.splice(cv.sel, 1); cv.sel = -1; renderCanvas(); return; }
  const b = e.target.closest("[data-kind],[data-size],[data-shape],#cv-send");
  if (!b) return;
  if (b.id === "cv-send") {
    copyText(cvText()).then(() => cvToast(C("copied"))).catch(() => cvToast(C("copyFail")));
    return;
  }
  if (b.dataset.kind) cv.kind = b.dataset.kind;
  if (b.dataset.size) [cv.w, cv.h] = b.dataset.size.split("x").map(Number);
  if (b.dataset.shape) { cv.shape = b.dataset.shape; cv.x = cv.y = 0; }
  renderCanvas();
});
document.addEventListener("input", (e) => {
  const id = e.target.id;
  if (id === "cv-w" || id === "cv-h") {
    const v = Math.max(5, Math.min(200, Number(e.target.value) || 0));
    if (v >= 5) { cv[id === "cv-w" ? "w" : "h"] = Math.round(v); renderStage(); }
  } else if (id === "cv-fsize") { cv.decor[cv.sel].s = Number(e.target.value); renderStage(); }
  else if (id === "cv-zoom") { cv.zoom = Number(e.target.value); moveImage(); }
  else if (id === "cv-note") cv.note = e.target.value;
  else if (id === "cv-name") cv.name = e.target.value;
});
document.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
  if (e.target.id === "cv-w" || e.target.id === "cv-h") renderCanvas();
  if (e.target.id === "cv-shape" && e.target.files[0]) { cv.shapePhoto = URL.createObjectURL(e.target.files[0]); renderCanvas(); return; }
  if (e.target.id !== "cv-file" || !e.target.files[0]) return;
  if (cv.photo) URL.revokeObjectURL(cv.photo);
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(e.target.files[0]);
  const im = new Image();
  im.onload = () => { cv.photo = url; cv.img = im; cv.x = cv.y = 0; cv.zoom = 1; renderCanvas(); };
  im.src = url;
});

/* drag the photo, scroll to zoom */
let pan = null;
document.addEventListener("pointerdown", (e) => {
  const svg = e.target.closest("#cv-stage svg");
  if (!svg) return;
  const dc = e.target.closest(".decor");
  if (dc) {
    const i = Number(dc.dataset.i), f = cv.decor[i], k = dims()[0] / svg.getBoundingClientRect().width;
    cv.sel = i;
    pan = { flower: f, sx: e.clientX, sy: e.clientY, ox: f.x, oy: f.y, k };
    document.querySelectorAll("#cv-stage .decor").forEach((el) => el.classList.toggle("sel", el === dc));
    svg.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    return;
  }
  if (!cv.img) return;
  pan = { sx: e.clientX, sy: e.clientY, ox: cv.x, oy: cv.y, k: dims()[0] / svg.getBoundingClientRect().width };
  svg.classList.add("drag");
  svg.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
});
document.addEventListener("pointermove", (e) => {
  if (!pan) return;
  if (pan.flower) {
    pan.flower.x = Math.round(pan.ox + (e.clientX - pan.sx) * pan.k);
    pan.flower.y = Math.round(pan.oy + (e.clientY - pan.sy) * pan.k);
    const el = document.querySelector(`#cv-stage .decor[data-i="${cv.sel}"]`);
    if (el) { el.setAttribute("x", pan.flower.x - pan.flower.s / 2); el.setAttribute("y", pan.flower.y - pan.flower.s / 2); }
    return;
  }
  cv.x = pan.ox + (e.clientX - pan.sx) * pan.k;
  cv.y = pan.oy + (e.clientY - pan.sy) * pan.k;
  moveImage();
});
const endPan = () => { const hadFlower = pan && pan.flower; pan = null; if (hadFlower) renderCanvas(); const s = document.querySelector("#cv-stage svg"); if (s) s.classList.remove("drag"); };
document.addEventListener("pointerup", endPan);
document.addEventListener("pointercancel", endPan);
document.addEventListener("wheel", (e) => {
  if (!cv.img || !e.target.closest("#cv-stage")) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  cv.zoom = Math.max(1, Math.min(4, cv.zoom + (e.deltaY < 0 ? 0.1 : -0.1)));
  const z = document.getElementById("cv-zoom");
  if (z) z.value = cv.zoom;
  moveImage();
}, { passive: false });

onLangChange(renderCanvas);
renderChrome();
renderCanvas();

/* drag the edges of the canvas to change its size; centimetres are always whole numbers */
let szDrag = null;
document.addEventListener("pointerdown", (e) => {
  const g = e.target.closest("[data-grip]"); if (!g) return;
  const r = document.querySelector("#sz-wrap svg").getBoundingClientRect();
  szDrag = { m: g.dataset.grip, x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, w: cv.w, h: cv.h, cmx: cv.w / r.width, cmy: cv.h / r.height };
  e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
}, true);
window.addEventListener("pointermove", (e) => {
  if (!szDrag) return;
  const clamp = (v) => Math.max(5, Math.min(200, Math.round(v)));
  if (szDrag.m !== "b") cv.w = clamp(szDrag.w + (e.clientX - szDrag.x) * szDrag.cmx);
  if (szDrag.m !== "r") cv.h = clamp(szDrag.h + (e.clientY - szDrag.y) * szDrag.cmy);
  const w = document.getElementById("cv-w"), h = document.getElementById("cv-h");
  if (w) w.value = cv.w; if (h) h.value = cv.h;
  renderStage();
});
window.addEventListener("pointerup", () => { if (szDrag) { szDrag = null; renderCanvas(); } });

const L = (k) => (TXT[ui.lang] || TXT.en)[k] || TXT.en[k];
const S = { w: 40, h: 50, bg: "#FFFFFF", items: [], sel: -1, name: "", note: "" };
const PX = 8;   // preview pixels per cm
const esc2 = (s) => String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, (c) => ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;" }[c]));
const HEART = (cx, cy, s) => { const k = s / 100; const P = [[0, 30], [-5, 8], [-35, 0], [-50, 22], [-50, 50], [0, 100], [50, 50], [50, 22], [35, 0], [5, 8], [0, 30]]; return ""; };
// heart path inside a w x h box at (x, y)
function heartPath(x, y, w, h) {
  const X = (u) => (x + (u / 100) * w).toFixed(1), Y = (v) => (y + (v / 100) * h).toFixed(1);
  return `M${X(50)} ${Y(96)} C${X(8)} ${Y(64)} ${X(-4)} ${Y(30)} ${X(18)} ${Y(12)} C${X(34)} ${Y(-1)} ${X(50)} ${Y(14)} ${X(50)} ${Y(26)} C${X(50)} ${Y(14)} ${X(66)} ${Y(-1)} ${X(82)} ${Y(12)} C${X(104)} ${Y(30)} ${X(92)} ${Y(64)} ${X(50)} ${Y(96)} Z`;
}
function clipFor(it, id) {
  const x = it.x - it.w / 2, y = it.y - it.h / 2;
  if (it.shape === "circle") return `<clipPath id="${id}"><ellipse cx="${it.x}" cy="${it.y}" rx="${it.w / 2}" ry="${it.h / 2}"/></clipPath>`;
  if (it.shape === "heart") return `<clipPath id="${id}"><path d="${heartPath(x, y, it.w, it.h)}"/></clipPath>`;
  return `<clipPath id="${id}"><rect x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${it.w}" height="${it.h}" rx="${it.shape === "rounded" ? Math.min(it.w, it.h) * 0.12 : 0}"/></clipPath>`;
}
function cover(it) {   // image rectangle that covers the box, times zoom
  const r = it.nw / it.nh, br = it.w / it.h;
  let iw, ih; if (r > br) { ih = it.h; iw = ih * r; } else { iw = it.w; ih = iw / r; }
  iw *= it.z; ih *= it.z;
  return [it.x - iw / 2, it.y - ih / 2, iw, ih];
}
function svg() {
  const defs = S.items.map((it, i) => clipFor(it, "c" + i)).join("");
  const body = S.items.map((it, i) => { const [ix, iy, iw, ih] = cover(it); return `<g class="ph" data-i="${i}" clip-path="url(#c${i})" style="cursor:move"><image href="${it.url}" x="${ix}" y="${iy}" width="${iw}" height="${ih}" preserveAspectRatio="none"/></g>`; }).join("");
  let sel = "";
  const it = S.items[S.sel];
  if (it) {
    const x = it.x - it.w / 2, y = it.y - it.h / 2;
    const hs = Math.max(S.w, S.h) * 0.018;
    sel = `<rect x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${it.w}" height="${it.h}" fill="none" stroke="#19708A" stroke-width="${hs / 4}" stroke-dasharray="${hs} ${hs / 2}" pointer-events="none"/><rect class="grip" x="${x + it.w - hs}" y="${y + it.h - hs}" width="${hs * 2}" height="${hs * 2}" rx="${hs / 3}" fill="#19708A" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="${hs / 5}" style="cursor:nwse-resize"/>`;
  }
  return `<div class="sz-wrap"><span class="sz-label">${Math.round(S.w)} × ${Math.round(S.h)} cm</span><i class="sz-grip r" data-grip="r"></i><i class="sz-grip b" data-grip="b"></i><i class="sz-grip c" data-grip="c"></i><svg id="cs" viewBox="0 0 ${S.w} ${S.h}" style="width:100%;max-width:${S.w * PX}px;height:auto;touch-action:none;background:${S.bg};box-shadow:0 12px 34px rgba(14,63,87,.22);border-radius:4px"><defs>${defs}</defs>${body}${sel}${S.items.length ? "" : `<text x="${S.w / 2}" y="${S.h / 2}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="${S.w / 32}" fill="#9AA">${esc2(L("empty"))}</text>`}</svg></div>`;
}
function chip(on, attr, label) { return `<button type="button" class="chip ${on ? "on" : ""}" aria-pressed="${on}" ${attr}>${label}</button>`; }
function render() {
  const it = S.items[S.sel];
  const root = document.getElementById("collage");
  root.innerHTML = `<section class="section"><h1>${L("title")}</h1><p class="lead">${L("sub")}</p>
  <div class="cl-grid">
    <div class="cl-stage" id="cl-stage">${svg()}</div>
    <div class="cl-panel">
      <div class="field"><span class="field-label">${L("size")}</span>
        <div class="cm-row"><label class="cm-field"><span>${L("w")}</span><input type="number" min="5" max="300" data-s="w" step="1" value="${S.w}"></label><label class="cm-field"><span>${L("h")}</span><input type="number" min="5" max="300" data-s="h" step="1" value="${S.h}"></label></div>
        <div class="chips">${[[20, 20], [30, 40], [40, 50], [50, 70], [60, 90]].map(([w, h]) => chip(S.w === w && S.h === h, `data-size="${w}x${h}"`, `${w} × ${h}`)).join("")}</div>
        <div class="chips">${chip(S.bg === "#FFFFFF", 'data-bg="#FFFFFF"', L("white"))}${chip(S.bg === "#F3E7D3", 'data-bg="#F3E7D3"', L("sand"))}</div></div>
      <div class="field"><span class="field-label">${L("add")}</span><input type="file" accept="image/*" multiple id="cl-file"><p class="small-note">${L("addHint")} (${S.items.length} ${L("count")})</p></div>
      <div class="field cl-sel"><span class="field-label">${L("sel")}</span>${it ? `
        <div class="chips">${["square", "rounded", "circle", "heart"].map((s) => chip(it.shape === s, `data-shape="${s}"`, L(s))).join("")}</div>
        <label class="cm-field"><span>${L("size2")}</span></label><input type="range" min="3" max="${Math.max(S.w, S.h)}" step="0.5" value="${it.w}" data-psize="1" style="width:100%;accent-color:#19708A">
        <label class="cm-field"><span>${L("zoom")}</span></label><input type="range" min="1" max="4" step="0.05" value="${it.z}" data-pzoom="1" style="width:100%;accent-color:#19708A">
        <div class="chips"><button type="button" class="chip" data-front="1">${L("front")}</button><button type="button" class="chip" data-del="1">${L("del")}</button></div>` : `<p class="small-note">${L("none")}</p>`}</div>
      <div class="field"><span class="field-label">${L("name")}</span><input type="text" id="cl-name" value="${esc2(S.name)}"></div>
      <div class="field"><span class="field-label">${L("note")}</span><textarea id="cl-note" rows="2">${esc2(S.note)}</textarea></div>
      <div class="send-row"><a class="btn btn-ink wide" id="cl-send" href="${igLink()}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${L("send")}</a></div>
    </div></div></section>`;
}
function redrawStage() { document.getElementById("cl-stage").innerHTML = svg(); }
function orderText() {
  const lines = ["Hi Anna! I'd like a photo canvas 💛", "", `Canvas size: ${S.w} × ${S.h} cm`, `Photos: ${S.items.length}`];
  S.items.forEach((it, i) => lines.push(`#${i + 1} ${it.name}: ${it.shape}, ${it.w.toFixed(1)} cm wide, at ${(it.x / S.w * 100).toFixed(0)}% across and ${(it.y / S.h * 100).toFixed(0)}% down`));
  if (S.name.trim()) lines.push("Name: " + S.name.trim());
  if (S.note.trim()) lines.push("Notes: " + S.note.trim());
  lines.push("(I'll attach my photos in this chat 📷)");
  return lines.join("\n");
}
function toastMsg(m) { const t = document.getElementById("toast"), x = document.getElementById("toast-text"); if (!t) return; x.textContent = m; t.classList.add("show"); clearTimeout(toastMsg._t); toastMsg._t = setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove("show"), 3200); }
const root = document.getElementById("collage");
root.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
  if (e.target.id === "cl-file") {
    [...e.target.files].forEach((f) => {
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(f), im = new Image();
      im.onload = () => {
        const base = Math.min(S.w, S.h) * 0.45, r = im.naturalWidth / im.naturalHeight;
        const n = S.items.length, w = base, h = base / Math.min(r, 1.6) * (r > 1 ? 1 : 1);
        S.items.push({ url, name: f.name.slice(0, 40), nw: im.naturalWidth, nh: im.naturalHeight, x: S.w / 2 + (n % 3 - 1) * base * 0.35, y: S.h / 2 + (n % 3 - 1) * base * 0.3, w, h: base, shape: "rounded", z: 1 });
        S.sel = S.items.length - 1; render();
      };
      im.src = url;
    });
    e.target.value = "";
  }
});
root.addEventListener("input", (e) => {
  const t = e.target, it = S.items[S.sel];
  if (t.dataset.s) { const v = Math.max(5, Math.min(300, Math.round(Number(t.value) || 0))); if (Number(t.value) >= 5) { S[t.dataset.s] = v; redrawStage(); } }
  else if (t.dataset.psize && it) { const r = it.h / it.w; it.w = Number(t.value); it.h = it.shape === "circle" || it.shape === "heart" ? it.w : it.w * r; redrawStage(); }
  else if (t.dataset.pzoom && it) { it.z = Number(t.value); redrawStage(); }
  else if (t.id === "cl-name") S.name = t.value;
  else if (t.id === "cl-note") S.note = t.value;
});
root.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
  const b = e.target.closest("button"), it = S.items[S.sel];
  if (e.target.closest("#cl-send")) { copyText(orderText()).then(() => toastMsg(L("copied"))).catch(() => {}); return; }
  if (!b) return;
  if (b.dataset.size) { const [w, h] = b.dataset.size.split("x").map(Number); S.w = w; S.h = h; render(); }
  else if (b.dataset.bg) { S.bg = b.dataset.bg; render(); }
  else if (b.dataset.shape && it) { it.shape = b.dataset.shape; if (it.shape === "circle" || it.shape === "heart") it.h = it.w; render(); }
  else if (b.dataset.front && it) { S.items.push(S.items.splice(S.sel, 1)[0]); S.sel = S.items.length - 1; render(); }
  else if (b.dataset.del && it) { URL.revokeObjectURL(it.url); S.items.splice(S.sel, 1); S.sel = -1; render(); }
});
// drag to move, drag the corner to resize
let drag = null;
root.addEventListener("pointerdown", (e) => {
  const svgEl = e.target.closest("#cs"); if (!svgEl) return;
  const pt = (ev) => { const r = svgEl.getBoundingClientRect(); return [(ev.clientX - r.left) / r.width * S.w, (ev.clientY - r.top) / r.height * S.h]; };
  const [px, py] = pt(e);
  if (e.target.classList.contains("grip")) { const it = S.items[S.sel]; drag = { mode: "size", it, svgEl, w0: it.w, h0: it.h, p0: [px, py] }; }
  else {
    const g = e.target.closest(".ph");
    if (!g) { S.sel = -1; render(); return; }
    const i = Number(g.dataset.i), it = S.items[i];
    if (S.sel !== i) { S.sel = i; const keep = document.getElementById("cl-stage"); render(); }
    drag = { mode: "move", it: S.items[i], svgEl: document.getElementById("cs"), d: [px - it.x, py - it.y] };
  }
  e.preventDefault();
});
window.addEventListener("pointermove", (e) => {
  if (!drag) return;
  const svgEl = document.getElementById("cs"), r = svgEl.getBoundingClientRect();
  const px = (e.clientX - r.left) / r.width * S.w, py = (e.clientY - r.top) / r.height * S.h, it = drag.it;
  if (drag.mode === "move") { it.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(S.w, px - drag.d[0])); it.y = Math.max(0, Math.min(S.h, py - drag.d[1])); }
  else { const k = Math.max(0.15, 1 + ((px - drag.p0[0]) + (py - drag.p0[1])) / (drag.w0 + drag.h0)); it.w = Math.max(2, drag.w0 * k); it.h = Math.max(2, drag.h0 * k); const sl = document.querySelector("[data-psize]"); if (sl) sl.value = it.w; }
  redrawStage();
});
window.addEventListener("pointerup", () => { drag = null; });
onLangChange && onLangChange(render);
renderChrome();
render();

/* drag the canvas edges to resize; whole centimetres only */
let szDrag = null;
root.addEventListener("pointerdown", (e) => {
  const g = e.target.closest("[data-grip]"); if (!g) return;
  const r = document.getElementById("cs").getBoundingClientRect();
  szDrag = { m: g.dataset.grip, x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, w: S.w, h: S.h, cmx: S.w / r.width, cmy: S.h / r.height };
  e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
}, true);
window.addEventListener("pointermove", (e) => {
  if (!szDrag) return;
  const clamp = (v) => Math.max(5, Math.min(300, Math.round(v)));
  if (szDrag.m !== "b") S.w = clamp(szDrag.w + (e.clientX - szDrag.x) * szDrag.cmx);
  if (szDrag.m !== "r") S.h = clamp(szDrag.h + (e.clientY - szDrag.y) * szDrag.cmy);
  const w = document.querySelector("[data-s=w]"), h = document.querySelector("[data-s=h]");
  if (w) w.value = S.w; if (h) h.value = S.h;
  redrawStage();
});
window.addEventListener("pointerup", () => { if (szDrag) { szDrag = null; render(); } });
